# encoding: utf-8
"""
AFD-ReID model: ResNet50 backbone + GeM/avg pool + BNNeck + ID classifier.

Baseline (use_afd=False) is a standard strong ReID baseline (Luo et al. BoT):
    resnet50(IMAGENET1K_V1) -> last_stride=1 -> pool -> BNNeck -> linear classifier
    train returns (global_feat, logits); test returns L2-normalized BN feature.

AFD modules (use_afd=True) -- altitude-frequency decoupling, both switchable:
  * Frequency Reliability Router (afd_router):
      split a shallow feature map into low / mid / high frequency bands via 2D FFT,
      predict per-band reliability weights with a light gate (optionally conditioned
      on view/altitude), and recombine the bands. The recombined feature replaces the
      input to the rest of the backbone. Designed to *down-weight unreliable high-freq*
      content under aerial (low-resolution) views.
  * Cross-View Frequency Counterfactual (afd_cvfc):
      training-time regularizer (no test-time cost). Two switchable sub-mechanisms:
        - high-band dropout: zero the high-freq band of the shallow feature with prob p,
          producing a counterfactual view that must keep the same identity.
        - low/high consistency: penalize the distance between the embedding of the full
          feature and the embedding of its low-pass counterfactual, so identity relies
          on the stable low/mid band shared across A<->G.
      The CVFC forward exposes counterfactual features; the losses live in afd_train.py
      so the mechanism stays cleanly ablatable.

Every AFD piece is gated by flags so `use_afd=False` reproduces the baseline exactly
(the AFD submodules are not even constructed when use_afd=False).
"""
import math

import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
import torchvision


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# weight init (BoT style)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def weights_init_kaiming(m):
    classname = m.__class__.__name__
    if classname.find('Linear') != -1:
        nn.init.kaiming_normal_(m.weight, a=0, mode='fan_out')
        if m.bias is not None:
            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)
    elif classname.find('Conv') != -1:
        nn.init.kaiming_normal_(m.weight, a=0, mode='fan_in')
        if m.bias is not None:
            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)
    elif classname.find('BatchNorm') != -1:
        if m.affine:
            nn.init.constant_(m.weight, 1.0)
            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)


def weights_init_classifier(m):
    classname = m.__class__.__name__
    if classname.find('Linear') != -1:
        nn.init.normal_(m.weight, std=0.001)
        if m.bias is not None:
            nn.init.constant_(m.bias, 0.0)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# GeM pooling
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
class GeMPool(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, p=3.0, eps=1e-6):
        super().__init__()
        self.p = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(1) * p)
        self.eps = eps

    def forward(self, x):
        x = x.clamp(min=self.eps).pow(self.p)
        x = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, 1).pow(1.0 / self.p)
        return x.flatten(1)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Frequency band decomposition (shared by Router and CVFC)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _band_masks(H, W, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, device='cpu', dtype=torch.float32):
    """Build (low, mid, high) centered rectangular FFT-shifted masks on an HxW grid.

    low  : central box of half-size low_r*(H,W)
    mid  : ring between low_r and mid_r
    high : everything outside mid_r
    Masks sum to 1 everywhere -> band partition is exact and lossless.
    """
    cy, cx = H // 2, W // 2
    ry1, rx1 = max(1, int(H * low_r)), max(1, int(W * low_r))
    ry2, rx2 = max(ry1 + 1, int(H * mid_r)), max(rx1 + 1, int(W * mid_r))

    low = torch.zeros(H, W, device=device, dtype=dtype)
    low[cy - ry1:cy + ry1, cx - rx1:cx + rx1] = 1.0

    midbox = torch.zeros(H, W, device=device, dtype=dtype)
    midbox[cy - ry2:cy + ry2, cx - rx2:cx + rx2] = 1.0
    mid = midbox - low                     # ring
    high = 1.0 - midbox                     # outside
    return low, mid, high


def decompose_bands(x, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30):
    """Decompose a feature map x:(B,C,H,W) into (low, mid, high) spatial-domain maps.

    Sum of the three equals x (up to fp error). Done per-sample via 2D FFT over (H,W).
    """
    B, C, H, W = x.shape
    f = torch.fft.fftshift(torch.fft.fft2(x.float(), dim=(-2, -1)), dim=(-2, -1))
    low_m, mid_m, high_m = _band_masks(H, W, low_r, mid_r, device=x.device)
    out = []
    for m in (low_m, mid_m, high_m):
        fm = f * m.view(1, 1, H, W)
        band = torch.fft.ifft2(torch.fft.ifftshift(fm, dim=(-2, -1)), dim=(-2, -1)).real
        out.append(band.to(x.dtype))
    return out  # [low, mid, high]


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# AFD Module 1: Frequency Reliability Router
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
class FrequencyReliabilityRouter(nn.Module):
    """Re-weight (low, mid, high) bands of a feature map by learned reliability.

    weight = softmax over 3 bands, optionally conditioned on view (aerial/ground).
    output = w_low*low + w_mid*mid + w_high*high   (residual-blended with input).

    cond_on_view: if True, the gate input is concatenated with a learned per-view
    embedding so aerial vs ground can learn different band reliabilities.
    """

    def __init__(self, channels, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, cond_on_view=True,
                 residual=True):
        super().__init__()
        self.low_r = low_r
        self.mid_r = mid_r
        self.cond_on_view = cond_on_view
        self.residual = residual

        gate_in = channels + (2 if cond_on_view else 0)
        hidden = max(channels // 8, 16)
        self.gate = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(gate_in, hidden),
            nn.ReLU(inplace=True),
            nn.Linear(hidden, 3),
        )
        if cond_on_view:
            # view embedding: index 0 = Aerial, 1 = Ground
            self.view_emb = nn.Embedding(2, 2)
            nn.init.zeros_(self.view_emb.weight)
        # init last gate layer near-zero so initial weights ~ uniform (stable start)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate[-1].weight)
        nn.init.zeros_(self.gate[-1].bias)

    def forward(self, x, view_idx=None):
        """x:(B,C,H,W); view_idx:(B,) long in {0,1} or None.

        Returns (recombined feature, band_weights(B,3)).
        """
        low, mid, high = decompose_bands(x, self.low_r, self.mid_r)
        ctx = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, 1).flatten(1)          # (B,C)
        if self.cond_on_view and view_idx is not None:
            ctx = torch.cat([ctx, self.view_emb(view_idx)], dim=1)
        elif self.cond_on_view:
            # no view at hand (e.g. some test paths) -> zero pad
            ctx = torch.cat([ctx, ctx.new_zeros(ctx.size(0), 2)], dim=1)
        w = torch.softmax(self.gate(ctx), dim=1)              # (B,3)
        wl, wm, wh = w[:, 0], w[:, 1], w[:, 2]

        def b(weight, band):
            return weight.view(-1, 1, 1, 1) * band

        recomb = b(wl, low) + b(wm, mid) + b(wh, high)
        # scale by 3 so that uniform weights (1/3 each) reproduce the input map
        recomb = recomb * 3.0
        if self.residual:
            recomb = 0.5 * x + 0.5 * recomb
        return recomb, w


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# AFD Module 2: Cross-View Frequency Counterfactual (training-time)
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
class CrossViewFrequencyCounterfactual(nn.Module):
    """Produce counterfactual feature maps for the consistency / dropout losses.

    Pure functional band ops -- no learnable params; kept as a module so the
    behavior is centralized and switchable. Used only in training.
    """

    def __init__(self, low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, high_drop_p=0.5):
        super().__init__()
        self.low_r = low_r
        self.mid_r = mid_r
        self.high_drop_p = high_drop_p

    def high_band_dropout(self, x):
        """Per-sample: with prob high_drop_p, drop the high band (keep low+mid)."""
        low, mid, high = decompose_bands(x, self.low_r, self.mid_r)
        B = x.size(0)
        keep = (torch.rand(B, device=x.device) >= self.high_drop_p).float()
        keep = keep.view(B, 1, 1, 1)
        return low + mid + keep * high

    def low_pass(self, x):
        """Keep only low+mid band (a stable, view-invariant counterfactual)."""
        low, mid, _ = decompose_bands(x, self.low_r, self.mid_r)
        return low + mid


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Main model
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
class AFDModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, num_classes, last_stride=1, pool='gem',
                 pretrained=True,
                 use_afd=False,
                 afd_router=True, afd_cvfc=True,
                 afd_stage='layer1',
                 router_cond_view=True,
                 low_r=0.125, mid_r=0.30, high_drop_p=0.5):
        super().__init__()
        self.use_afd = use_afd
        self.afd_router = use_afd and afd_router
        self.afd_cvfc = use_afd and afd_cvfc
        self.afd_stage = afd_stage
        self.in_planes = 2048

        weights = 'IMAGENET1K_V1' if pretrained else None
        resnet = torchvision.models.resnet50(weights=weights)
        # ReID standard: last block stride 1 -> larger spatial map (16x8 for 256x128)
        if last_stride == 1:
            resnet.layer4[0].conv2.stride = (1, 1)
            resnet.layer4[0].downsample[0].stride = (1, 1)

        # split backbone so the router can be inserted after a shallow stage
        self.stem = nn.Sequential(
            resnet.conv1, resnet.bn1, resnet.relu, resnet.maxpool)
        self.layer1 = resnet.layer1   # 256 ch
        self.layer2 = resnet.layer2   # 512 ch
        self.layer3 = resnet.layer3   # 1024 ch
        self.layer4 = resnet.layer4   # 2048 ch

        # channel count at the chosen insertion stage
        stage_ch = {'stem': 64, 'layer1': 256, 'layer2': 512}
        assert afd_stage in stage_ch, f"afd_stage must be one of {list(stage_ch)}"
        self.router_channels = stage_ch[afd_stage]

        if self.afd_router:
            self.router = FrequencyReliabilityRouter(
                self.router_channels, low_r=low_r, mid_r=mid_r,
                cond_on_view=router_cond_view)
        if self.afd_cvfc:
            self.cvfc = CrossViewFrequencyCounterfactual(
                low_r=low_r, mid_r=mid_r, high_drop_p=high_drop_p)

        # pooling
        self.pool = GeMPool() if pool == 'gem' else None  # None -> avg in forward

        # BNNeck
        self.bottleneck = nn.BatchNorm1d(self.in_planes)
        self.bottleneck.bias.requires_grad_(False)
        self.bottleneck.apply(weights_init_kaiming)

        self.classifier = nn.Linear(self.in_planes, num_classes, bias=False)
        self.classifier.apply(weights_init_classifier)

    # --- backbone forward with optional router insertion ------------------- #
    def _forward_backbone(self, x, view_idx=None, feat_override=None,
                          insert_router=False):
        """Run stem->layer4. If insert_router, apply router at self.afd_stage.

        feat_override: if given, a dict {stage: tensor} used to *replace* the
        feature at that stage (for counterfactual passes that re-enter mid-network).
        """
        band_w = None
        x = self.stem(x)
        if self.afd_stage == 'stem':
            x = self._maybe_route(x, 'stem', view_idx, insert_router)
            x, band_w = x if isinstance(x, tuple) else (x, band_w)

        x = self.layer1(x)
        if self.afd_stage == 'layer1':
            x = self._maybe_route(x, 'layer1', view_idx, insert_router)
            x, band_w = x if isinstance(x, tuple) else (x, band_w)

        x = self.layer2(x)
        if self.afd_stage == 'layer2':
            x = self._maybe_route(x, 'layer2', view_idx, insert_router)
            x, band_w = x if isinstance(x, tuple) else (x, band_w)

        x = self.layer3(x)
        x = self.layer4(x)
        return x, band_w

    def _maybe_route(self, x, stage, view_idx, insert_router):
        if insert_router and self.afd_router and stage == self.afd_stage:
            return self.router(x, view_idx)   # returns (feat, w)
        return x

    def _pool(self, x):
        if self.pool is not None:
            return self.pool(x)
        return F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(x, 1).flatten(1)

    def _embed(self, x):
        """global feat -> BNNeck feat."""
        g = self._pool(x)
        bn = self.bottleneck(g)
        return g, bn

    # --- public forward ---------------------------------------------------- #
    def forward(self, x, view_idx=None, return_cvfc=False):
        """
        Train: returns dict with global_feat, bn_feat, logits, band_w,
               and (if return_cvfc & afd_cvfc) counterfactual embeddings.
        Eval : returns L2-normalized BN feature.
        """
        feat_map, band_w = self._forward_backbone(
            x, view_idx=view_idx, insert_router=self.afd_router)
        global_feat, bn_feat = self._embed(feat_map)

        if not self.training:
            return F.normalize(bn_feat, dim=1)

        out = {
            'global_feat': global_feat,   # for triplet (before BN)
            'bn_feat': bn_feat,           # BN feature
            'logits': self.classifier(bn_feat),
            'band_w': band_w,             # (B,3) or None
        }

        if return_cvfc and self.afd_cvfc:
            # build counterfactual at the SHALLOW stage, then run remainder.
            # We re-extract the shallow feature, apply CVFC, and finish forward.
            cf = self._forward_counterfactual(x, view_idx)
            out.update(cf)
        return out

    def _forward_counterfactual(self, x, view_idx):
        """Run two cheap counterfactual passes (high-drop, low-pass) from the
        shallow stage onward, returning their BN embeddings for consistency loss."""
        # get shallow feature up to (and including) the insertion stage
        stage = self.afd_stage
        shallow = self.stem(x)
        if stage in ('layer1', 'layer2'):
            shallow = self.layer1(shallow)
        if stage == 'layer2':
            shallow = self.layer2(shallow)

        def finish(feat):
            # continue from the stage AFTER the insertion point
            if stage == 'stem':
                h = self.layer1(feat); h = self.layer2(h)
            elif stage == 'layer1':
                h = self.layer2(feat)
            else:  # layer2
                h = feat
            h = self.layer3(h); h = self.layer4(h)

     1	# encoding: utf-8
     2	"""
     3	CVPB kill-switch #1 -- zero-training local-token MaxSim probe on CARGO.
     4	
     5	Idea
     6	----
     7	Load the *baseline* (use_afd=False) ResNet50-BoT checkpoint, extract the
     8	layer4 spatial feature map (the GeM/pool input, 16x8 for 256x128) for every
     9	query / gallery image, flatten it into K local "evidence" tokens, and ask:
    10	
    11	    does a global-cosine + bidirectional MaxSim(tokens) hybrid beat plain
    12	    global-cosine on cross-view A<->G retrieval?
    13	
    14	If hybrid > global by +0.5~1.0 mAP, local set-matching carries real
    15	cross-view evidence and the CVPB "Local Token MaxSim" module is worth
    16	training. If not, drop the module (empirical kill-switch, no confound test).
    17	
    18	Scoring (made explicit; this is the load-bearing design choice)
    19	---------------------------------------------------------------
    20	Ranking is done on a *combined similarity* (higher = better match):
    21	
    22	    s_global(q,g) = cos(global_bn_q, global_bn_g)            # baseline score
    23	    s_maxsim(q,g) = 0.5 * ( mean_k max_j cos(qtok_k, gtok_j)
    24	                          + mean_j max_k cos(gtok_j, qtok_k) ) # bidirectional
    25	    s_hybrid      = s_global + beta * s_maxsim
    26	
    27	We rank by descending s_hybrid (equivalently distance = -s_hybrid).  This is
    28	the standard MaxSim-rerank form; adding a *distance* and a *similarity*
    29	together (as a literal reading of "global_cos + beta*maxsim") would mix signs
    30	and is not what gives a meaningful delta, so we combine in similarity space
    31	and rank consistently.  beta=0 reproduces the baseline global-cosine ranking,
    32	which we use to verify the pipeline reproduces ~32.48 mAP.
    33	
    34	All tokens are L2-normalized.  global_bn is the model's eval-mode output
    35	(already L2-normalized BNNeck feature), so s_global here is identical to the
    36	cosine similarity the training eval uses (eval_market ranks by 2-2*cos, i.e.
    37	ascending cos-distance == descending cos-sim -> same order).
    38	
    39	Run on lab-3090:
    40	    cd /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb
    41	    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python maxsim_probe.py \
    42	        --ckpt /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/log/cargo/afd_baseline/model_best.pth \
    43	        2>&1 | tee /tmp/cvpb_maxsim_probe.log
    44	"""

    58	                                '..', 'afd_reid'))

    64	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    65	# feature extraction: global BN feature + layer4 spatial token map
    66	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    67	class TokenExtractor:
    68	    """Hooks model.layer4 to grab its (B,2048,H,W) output during a normal
    69	    eval forward, alongside the model's returned L2-normalized BN feature."""
    70	
    71	    def __init__(self, model):
    72	        self.model = model
    73	        self._buf = {}
    74	        # layer4 output == the map fed into GeM pool in AFDModel
    75	        self.handle = model.layer4.register_forward_hook(self._hook)
    76	
    77	    def _hook(self, module, inp, out):
    78	        self._buf['map'] = out.detach()
    79	
    80	    def remove(self):
    81	        self.handle.remove()
    82	
    83	    @torch.no_grad()
    84	    def run(self, loader, device, pool_grid=None):
    85	        """Return (global_bn[N,2048], tokens[N,K,2048] L2-normed, pids, camids).
    86	
    87	        pool_grid: None -> use full layer4 grid (K = H*W). Else (gh,gw) ->
    88	        adaptive_avg_pool2d the map to gh x gw before tokenizing (K = gh*gw).
    89	        """
    90	        self.model.eval()
    91	        g_list, t_list, p_list, c_list = [], [], [], []
    92	        for batch in loader:
    93	            imgs = batch['img'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
    94	            # baseline: use_afd=False -> view_idx unused; eval returns normed BN feat
    95	            gfeat = self.model(imgs)                     # (B,2048) L2-normed
    96	            fmap = self._buf['map']                       # (B,2048,H,W)
    97	            if pool_grid is not None:
    98	                fmap = F.adaptive_avg_pool2d(fmap, pool_grid)
    99	            B, C, H, W = fmap.shape
   100	            tok = fmap.flatten(2).permute(0, 2, 1).contiguous()  # (B,K,C)
   101	            tok = F.normalize(tok, dim=2)                 # per-token L2
   102	            g_list.append(gfeat.cpu())
   103	            t_list.append(tok.cpu())
   104	            p_list.append(batch['pid'])
   105	            c_list.append(batch['camid'])
   106	        if not g_list:
   107	            return (torch.empty(0), torch.empty(0),
   108	                    np.empty(0, np.int64), np.empty(0, np.int64))
   109	        g = torch.cat(g_list, 0)
   110	        t = torch.cat(t_list, 0)
   111	        p = torch.cat(p_list, 0).numpy()
   112	        c = torch.cat(c_list, 0).numpy()
   113	        return g, t, p, c
   114	
   115	
   116	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   117	# similarity matrices
   118	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   119	def global_sim(qg, gg):
   120	    """Cosine similarity between L2-normed global feats -> (num_q,num_g)."""
   121	    return (qg @ gg.t())
   122	
   123	
   124	@torch.no_grad()
   125	def maxsim_bidir(qt, gt, device, mem_budget_elems=120_000_000):
   126	    """Bidirectional MaxSim similarity between token sets.
   127	
   128	    qt:(Nq,Kq,C) gt:(Ng,Kg,C), tokens already L2-normed.
   129	    s(q,g) = 0.5*( mean_k max_j <qtok_k,gtok_j> + mean_j max_k <gtok_j,qtok_k> )
   130	    Returns (Nq,Ng) on cpu.
   131	
   132	    Galleries are huge here (Ng up to ~32k) and queries tiny (~100-200), so the
   133	    cost is dominated by Ng.  We keep ALL queries on-GPU and chunk over the
   134	    GALLERY axis, building a (Nq,Kq,Gblk,Kg) sim block per chunk and reducing it
   135	    immediately.  Gblk is chosen so the block has <= mem_budget_elems floats.
   136	    """
   137	    Nq, Kq, C = qt.shape
   138	    Ng, Kg, _ = gt.shape
   139	    qt_d = qt.to(device)                              # (Nq,Kq,C) small
   140	    qt_flat = qt_d.reshape(Nq * Kq, C)                # (Nq*Kq,C)
   141	    # block size so Nq*Kq*Gblk*Kg <= budget (and at least 1)
   142	    per_g = max(1, Nq * Kq * Kg)
   143	    gblk = max(1, min(Ng, mem_budget_elems // per_g))
   144	    out = torch.empty(Nq, Ng)
   145	    for s in range(0, Ng, gblk):
   146	        e = min(s + gblk, Ng)
   147	        gc = gt[s:e].to(device)                       # (g,Kg,C)
   148	        g = gc.size(0)
   149	        gc_flat = gc.reshape(g * Kg, C)               # (g*Kg,C)
   150	        # (Nq*Kq, g*Kg) -> (Nq,Kq,g,Kg)
   151	        sim = (qt_flat @ gc_flat.t()).reshape(Nq, Kq, g, Kg)
   152	        q2g = sim.max(dim=3).values.mean(dim=1)       # (Nq,g) q-token best in g
   153	        g2q = sim.max(dim=1).values.mean(dim=2)       # (Nq,g) g-token best in q
   154	        out[:, s:e] = (0.5 * (q2g + g2q)).cpu()
   155	        del sim, q2g, g2q, gc, gc_flat
   156	    del qt_d, qt_flat
   157	    if device == 'cuda':
   158	        torch.cuda.empty_cache()
   159	    return out
   160	
   161	
   162	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   163	# market-style mAP/CMC from a precomputed *distance* matrix
   164	# (same junk removal as afd_train.eval_market; lower distance == better)
   165	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   166	def eval_from_distmat(distmat, q_pids, q_camids, g_pids, g_camids, max_rank=50):
   167	    if distmat.size == 0:
   168	        return float('nan'), float('nan'), float('nan')
   169	    num_q, num_g = distmat.shape
   170	    max_rank = min(max_rank, num_g)
   171	    indices = np.argsort(distmat, axis=1)
   172	    matches = (g_pids[indices] == q_pids[:, np.newaxis]).astype(np.int32)
   173	
   174	    all_cmc, all_AP, all_INP = [], [], []
   175	    num_valid_q = 0
   176	    for q_idx in range(num_q):
   177	        q_pid = q_pids[q_idx]
   178	        q_camid = q_camids[q_idx]
   179	        order = indices[q_idx]
   180	        remove = (g_pids[order] == q_pid) & (g_camids[order] == q_camid)
   181	        keep = np.invert(remove)
   182	        raw_cmc = matches[q_idx][keep]
   183	        if not np.any(raw_cmc):
   184	            continue
   185	        cmc = raw_cmc.cumsum()
   186	        pos_idx = np.where(raw_cmc == 1)[0]
   187	        max_pos_idx = pos_idx[-1]
   188	        all_INP.append(cmc[max_pos_idx] / (max_pos_idx + 1.0))
   189	        cmc_clip = cmc.copy()
   190	        cmc_clip[cmc_clip > 1] = 1
   191	        all_cmc.append(cmc_clip[:max_rank])
   192	        num_valid_q += 1
   193	        num_rel = raw_cmc.sum()
   194	        tmp = raw_cmc.cumsum()
   195	        tmp = [x / (i + 1.0) for i, x in enumerate(tmp)]
   196	        tmp = np.asarray(tmp) * raw_cmc
   197	        all_AP.append(tmp.sum() / num_rel)
   198	    if num_valid_q == 0:
   199	        return float('nan'), float('nan'), float('nan')
   200	    cmc = np.asarray(all_cmc).astype(np.float32).sum(0) / num_valid_q
   201	    return float(np.mean(all_AP)) * 100, float(cmc[0]) * 100, float(np.mean(all_INP)) * 100
   202	
   203

   211	                      num_workers=args.workers, pin_memory=True)

   297	
   298	        per_dir = {}   # tag -> (gsim, msim, qp, qc, gp, gc)
   299	        for tag, q, g in directions:
   300	            ql = build_loader(q, args)
   301	            gl = build_loader(g, args)
   302	            qg, qt, qp, qc = extractor.run(ql, device, pool_grid=pg)
   303	            gg, gt, gp, gc = extractor.run(gl, device, pool_grid=pg)
   304	            gsim = global_sim(qg, gg).numpy()                      # (Nq,Ng)
   305	            msim = maxsim_bidir(qt, gt, device).numpy()            # (Nq,Ng)
   306	            per_dir[tag] = (gsim, msim, qp, qc, gp, gc)
   307	
   308	            # baseline global-only (rank by -gsim == cosine distance order)
   309	            if glabel not in base_map:
   310	                base_map[glabel] = {}
   311	            bmap, br1, _ = eval_from_distmat(-gsim, qp, qc, gp, gc)
   312	            base_map[glabel][tag] = (bmap, br1)
   313	            print(f'  [{tag}] global-only  mAP={bmap:.2f}  R1={br1:.2f}  '
   314	                  f'(Nq={len(qp)} Ng={len(gp)})')
   315	
   316	        # baseline mean for this grid (global feats are grid-independent ->
   317	        # identical across grids; we print per-grid for sanity)
   318	        bmean = np.mean([base_map[glabel][t][0] for t in ('A->G', 'G->A')])
   319	        print(f'  [mean] global-only mAP={bmean:.2f}')
   320	
   321	        for beta in args.betas:
   322	            row = {}
   323	            for tag, q, g in directions:
   324	                gsim, msim, qp, qc, gp, gc = per_dir[tag]
   325	                hyb = gsim + beta * msim                # combined similarity
   326	                mAP, r1, minp = eval_from_distmat(-hyb, qp, qc, gp, gc)
   327	                row[tag] = (mAP, r1, minp)
   328	            mean_map = np.mean([row[t][0] for t in ('A->G', 'G->A')])
   329	            mean_r1 = np.mean([row[t][1] for t in ('A->G', 'G->A')])
   330	            results[(glabel, beta)] = (row, mean_map, mean_r1)
   331	            print(f'  beta={beta:<4} | A->G mAP={row["A->G"][0]:6.2f} R1={row["A->G"][1]:6.2f}'
   332	                  f' | G->A mAP={row["G->A"][0]:6.2f} R1={row["G->A"][1]:6.2f}'
   333	                  f' | MEAN mAP={mean_map:6.2f} R1={mean_r1:6.2f}')
   334	
   335	    extractor.remove()
   336	
   337	    # ---- summary table ----
   338	    print('\n' + '=' * 72)
   339	    print('SUMMARY  (mean A<->G mAP; baseline global-only target ~= 32.48)')
   340	    print('=' * 72)
   341	    any_grid = grid_label(pool_grids[0])
   342	    gbase_mean = np.mean([base_map[any_grid][t][0] for t in ('A->G', 'G->A')])
   343	    print(f'{"grid":>12} | {"global":>7} | ' +
   344	          ' | '.join(f'b={b:<4}' for b in args.betas))
   345	    for pg in pool_grids:
   346	        glabel = grid_label(pg)
   347	        gb = np.mean([base_map[glabel][t][0] for t in ('A->G', 'G->A')])
   348	        cells = []
   349	        for b in args.betas:
   350	            mean_map = results[(glabel, b)][1]
   351	            cells.append(f'{mean_map:6.2f}')
   352	        print(f'{glabel:>12} | {gb:7.2f} | ' + ' | '.join(cells))
   353	
   354	    # ---- verdict ----
   355	    best_key = max(results, key=lambda k: results[k][1])
   356	    best_mean = results[best_key][1]
   357	    delta = best_mean - gbase_mean
   358	    print('-' * 72)
   359	    print(f'baseline global-only mean mAP = {gbase_mean:.2f}')
   360	    print(f'best hybrid = grid {best_key[0]} beta {best_key[1]} -> '
   361	          f'mean mAP {best_mean:.2f}  (delta {delta:+.2f})')
   362	    if delta >= 0.5:
   363	        print(f'VERDICT: PASS  (+{delta:.2f} >= +0.5) -> local MaxSim carries '
   364	              f'cross-view evidence; train the Local Token MaxSim module.')
   365	    else:
   366	        print(f'VERDICT: FAIL  ({delta:+.2f} < +0.5) -> local MaxSim adds little '
   367	              f'on the trained baseline; drop/replace the module.')
   368	    print('=' * 72)
   369	
   370	
   371	if __name__ == '__main__':
   372	    main()
     1	# encoding: utf-8
     2	"""
     3	CVPB training loop on CARGO = the afd_reid baseline trainer + OVP-Mem module.
     4	
     5	This reuses the dataset / model / eval from ../afd_reid unchanged.  The only
     6	addition vs afd_reid/afd_train.py is the **OVP-Mem (Opposite-View Prototype
     7	Memory)** option, gated by --ovp (default OFF -> reproduces the BoT baseline).
     8	
     9	Baseline (no flags):
    10	    resnet50(IMAGENET1K_V1) + GeM + BNNeck
    11	    loss = CE(label-smooth 0.1) + batch-hard triplet (margin 0.3)
    12	    PK sampler P=16 x K=4 (bs=64), AdamW lr 3.5e-4, 10-ep warmup + cosine, 60 ep.
    13	    eval every 10 ep: A->G and G->A cross-view mAP / R1 / mINP.
    14	
    15	--ovp (OVP-Mem):
    16	    Maintain, per train pid, two EMA prototypes (aerial / ground) of the
    17	    L2-normalized BNNeck feature in a register_buffer of shape
    18	    [num_pid, 2, feat_dim]; EMA momentum 0.2.  Each step:
    19	      1) update the prototypes of the pids/views present in the batch (EMA),
    20	      2) add an InfoNCE loss pulling each sample toward its OWN pid's
    21	         OPPOSITE-view prototype and away from all other pids' opposite-view
    22	         prototypes:  CE( cos(z, P[:, opp_view]) / tau ,  y ).
    23	    total = CE + triplet + lambda_ovp * OVP.   (batch size unchanged, bs=64.)
    24	
    25	    Cold-start handling: a sample only contributes to the OVP loss once its own
    26	    opposite-view prototype has been initialized (seen >=1 time); candidate
    27	    columns that are still uninitialized are masked out of the InfoNCE logits so
    28	    a zero prototype can never act as an easy negative/positive.
    29	
    30	Run on lab-3090:
    31	    cd /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/experiments/cargo_cvpb
    32	    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 python afd_train.py \
    33	        --data_root /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data \
    34	        --out_dir   /root/work/SOLIDER-REID/log/cargo/cvpb_ovp \
    35	        --ovp \
    36	        2>&1 | tee /tmp/cvpb_ovp.log
    37	    # baseline reproduction: drop --ovp
    38	"""
    39	import os
    40	import sys
    41	import time
    42	import math
    43	import random
    44	import argparse
    45	
    46	import numpy as np
    47	import torch
    48	import torch.nn as nn
    49	import torch.nn.functional as F
    50	from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
    51	
    52	# reuse afd_reid building blocks unchanged
    53	sys.path.insert(0, os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)),
    54	                                '..', 'afd_reid'))
    55	from cargo_dataset import (CARGO, CARGOImageDataset, build_transforms,  # noqa: E402
    56	                           RandomIdentitySampler, filter_by_view)
    57	from afd_model import build_model  # noqa: E402
    58	# reuse the exact loss / schedule / eval helpers so nothing diverges
    59	from afd_train import (CrossEntropyLabelSmooth, TripletLoss, WarmupCosineLR,  # noqa: E402
    60	                       run_cross_view_eval, print_eval, set_seed)
    61	
    62	
    63	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    64	# OVP-Mem: opposite-view prototype memory
    65	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    66	class OVPMemory(nn.Module):
    67	    """Per-pid, per-view EMA prototype bank + opposite-view InfoNCE loss.
    68	
    69	    bank: register_buffer [num_pid, 2, feat_dim]  (view 0 = Aerial, 1 = Ground)
    70	    init: register_buffer [num_pid, 2] uint8 = has this (pid,view) been seen.
    71	
    72	    Prototypes are L2-normalized; features used for update/loss are the
    73	    L2-normalized BNNeck features (detached for the EMA update).
    74	    """
    75	
    76	    def __init__(self, num_pid, feat_dim, momentum=0.2, tau=0.05):
    77	        super().__init__()
    78	        self.num_pid = num_pid
    79	        self.feat_dim = feat_dim
    80	        self.momentum = momentum          # weight on the *new* batch mean
    81	        self.tau = tau
    82	        self.register_buffer('bank', torch.zeros(num_pid, 2, feat_dim))
    83	        self.register_buffer('inited', torch.zeros(num_pid, 2, dtype=torch.uint8))
    84	
    85	    @torch.no_grad()
    86	    def update(self, feats, labels, views):
    87	        """EMA-update prototypes for the (pid,view) groups present in the batch.
    88	
    89	        feats:(B,D) L2-normed BN feats (detached). labels:(B,) long.
    90	        views:(B,) long in {0,1}.  Per group, EMA toward the group mean, then
    91	        re-normalize the prototype.
    92	        """
    93	        feats = feats.detach()
    94	        for v in (0, 1):
    95	            vmask = (views == v)
    96	            if not torch.any(vmask):
    97	                continue
    98	            fv = feats[vmask]
    99	            lv = labels[vmask]
   100	            uniq = torch.unique(lv)
   101	            for pid in uniq:
   102	                pid_i = int(pid)
   103	                gmean = fv[lv == pid].mean(dim=0)
   104	                gmean = F.normalize(gmean, dim=0)
   105	                if self.inited[pid_i, v] == 0:
   106	                    self.bank[pid_i, v] = gmean
   107	                    self.inited[pid_i, v] = 1
   108	                else:
   109	                    self.bank[pid_i, v] = ((1 - self.momentum) * self.bank[pid_i, v]
   110	                                           + self.momentum * gmean)
   111	                    self.bank[pid_i, v] = F.normalize(self.bank[pid_i, v], dim=0)
   112	
   113	    def loss(self, feats, labels, views):
   114	        """InfoNCE: each sample classified against ALL pids' opposite-view
   115	        prototypes, target = its own pid.  feats:(B,D) L2-normed BN feats
   116	        (NOT detached -> gradient flows to the encoder).
   117	
   118	        Returns a scalar loss (0 if no sample has an initialized own-opp proto).
   119	        """
   120	        device = feats.device
   121	        opp = 1 - views                                   # opposite view per sample
   122	        total = feats.new_zeros(())
   123	        count = 0
   124	        for v_opp in (0, 1):
   125	            # samples whose opposite view == v_opp (i.e. samples from view 1-v_opp)
   126	            smask = (opp == v_opp)
   127	            if not torch.any(smask):
   128	                continue
   129	            protos = self.bank[:, v_opp, :]               # (num_pid, D)
   130	            valid = (self.inited[:, v_opp] == 1)          # (num_pid,) candidate cols
   131	            if valid.sum() == 0:
   132	                continue
   133	            z = feats[smask]                              # (b,D)
   134	            y = labels[smask]                             # (b,)
   135	            # only keep samples whose OWN opposite-view proto exists
   136	            own_ok = valid[y]
   137	            if own_ok.sum() == 0:
   138	                continue
   139	            z = z[own_ok]
   140	            y = y[own_ok]
   141	            logits = (z @ protos.t()) / self.tau          # (b, num_pid)
   142	            # mask out uninitialized candidate columns
   143	            logits = logits.masked_fill(~valid.view(1, -1), float('-inf'))
   144	            total = total + F.cross_entropy(logits, y)
   145	            count += 1
   146	        if count == 0:
   147	            return feats.new_zeros(())
   148	        return total / count
   149	
   150	
   151	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   152	# train
   153	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
   154	def main():
   155	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
   156	    ap.add_argument('--data_root', default='/root/work/SOLIDER-REID/data')
   157	    ap.add_argument('--out_dir', default='./log/cargo/cvpb_ovp')
   158	    ap.add_argument('--img_size', type=int, nargs=2, default=[256, 128])
   159	    ap.add_argument('--epochs', type=int, default=60)
   160	    ap.add_argument('--warmup_epochs', type=int, default=10)
   161	    ap.add_argument('--lr', type=float, default=3.5e-4)
   162	    ap.add_argument('--weight_decay', type=float, default=5e-4)
   163	    ap.add_argument('--P', type=int, default=16)
   164	    ap.add_argument('--K', type=int, default=4)
   165	    ap.add_argument('--test_batch', type=int, default=128)
   166	    ap.add_argument('--workers', type=int, default=8)
   167	    ap.add_argument('--margin', type=float, default=0.3)
   168	    ap.add_argument('--label_smooth', type=float, default=0.1)
   169	    ap.add_argument('--eval_period', type=int, default=10)
   170	    ap.add_argument('--seed', type=int, default=1)
   171	    ap.add_argument('--no_amp', action='store_true', help='disable mixed precision')
   172	    # model switches (keep AFD off by default -> pure BoT baseline + OVP)
   173	    ap.add_argument('--last_stride', type=int, default=1)
   174	    ap.add_argument('--pool', default='gem', choices=['gem', 'avg'])
   175	    ap.add_argument('--use_afd', action='store_true')
   176	    ap.add_argument('--afd_router', type=int, default=1)
   177	    ap.add_argument('--afd_cvfc', type=int, default=1)
   178	    ap.add_argument('--afd_stage', default='layer1',
   179	                    choices=['stem', 'layer1', 'layer2'])
   180	    ap.add_argument('--router_cond_view', type=int, default=1)
   181	    ap.add_argument('--low_r', type=float, default=0.125)
   182	    ap.add_argument('--mid_r', type=float, default=0.30)
   183	    ap.add_argument('--high_drop_p', type=float, default=0.5)
   184	    ap.add_argument('--w_cvfc', type=float, default=0.5)
   185	    # --- OVP-Mem ---
   186	    ap.add_argument('--ovp', action='store_true',
   187	                    help='enable OVP-Mem opposite-view prototype InfoNCE loss')
   188	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_lambda', type=float, default=0.5,
   189	                    help='weight of the OVP InfoNCE loss')
   190	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_tau', type=float, default=0.05,
   191	                    help='temperature for the OVP InfoNCE logits')
   192	    ap.add_argument('--ovp_momentum', type=float, default=0.2,
   193	                    help='EMA momentum = weight on the new batch mean')
   194	    args = ap.parse_args()
   195	    args.afd_router = bool(args.afd_router)
   196	    args.afd_cvfc = bool(args.afd_cvfc)
   197	    args.router_cond_view = bool(args.router_cond_view)
   198	
   199	    set_seed(args.seed)
   200	    os.makedirs(args.out_dir, exist_ok=True)
   201	    device = 'cuda'
   202	    batch_size = args.P * args.K
   203	
   204	    print("=" * 70)
   205	    print("CVPB-ReID training (afd_reid baseline + OVP-Mem)")
   206	    print(f"  use_afd={args.use_afd}  ovp={args.ovp} "
   207	          f"(lambda={args.ovp_lambda} tau={args.ovp_tau} mom={args.ovp_momentum})")
   208	    print(f"  bs={batch_size} (P={args.P} K={args.K}) lr={args.lr} "
   209	          f"epochs={args.epochs} warmup={args.warmup_epochs} amp={not args.no_amp}")
   210	    print(f"  out_dir={args.out_dir}")
   211	    print("=" * 70)
   212	
   213	    # data
   214	    dataset = CARGO(root=args.data_root, verbose=True)
   215	    train_tf = build_transforms(is_train=True, img_size=tuple(args.img_size))
   216	    train_set = CARGOImageDataset(dataset.train, train_tf)
   217	    sampler = RandomIdentitySampler(dataset.train, batch_size, args.K)
   218	    train_loader = DataLoader(train_set, batch_size=batch_size, sampler=sampler,
   219	                              num_workers=args.workers, pin_memory=True,
   220	                              drop_last=True)
   221	
   222	    # model
   223	    model = build_model(dataset.num_train_pids, args).to(device)
   224	
   225	    # losses
   226	    ce = CrossEntropyLabelSmooth(dataset.num_train_pids, args.label_smooth)
   227	    tri = TripletLoss(args.margin)
   228	
   229	    # OVP memory (buffers live on the model device; not optimized)
   230	    ovp = None
   231	    if args.ovp:
   232	        ovp = OVPMemory(dataset.num_train_pids, model.in_planes,
   233	                        momentum=args.ovp_momentum, tau=args.ovp_tau).to(device)
   234	
   235	    # optimizer
   236	    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=args.lr,
   237	                                  weight_decay=args.weight_decay)
   238	    scheduler = WarmupCosineLR(optimizer, args.warmup_epochs, args.epochs)
   239	    scaler = torch.amp.GradScaler('cuda', enabled=not args.no_amp)
   240	
   241	    view_map = {'Aerial': 0, 'Ground': 1}
   242	    best_map = -1.0
   243	    best_epoch = -1
   244	    n_iter_total = len(train_loader)
   245	
   246	    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):
   247	        model.train()
   248	        t0 = time.time()
   249	        meters = {'loss': 0.0, 'ce': 0.0, 'tri': 0.0, 'ovp': 0.0, 'acc': 0.0}
   250	        seen = 0
   251	
   252	        for it, batch in enumerate(train_loader):
   253	            imgs = batch['img'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   254	            labels = batch['pid'].to(device, non_blocking=True)
   255	            views = torch.tensor([view_map[v] for v in batch['view']],
   256	                                 device=device)
   257	            vidx = views if args.use_afd else None
   258	
   259	            optimizer.zero_grad()
   260	            with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=not args.no_amp):
   261	                out = model(imgs, view_idx=vidx,
   262	                            return_cvfc=(args.use_afd and args.afd_cvfc))
   263	                logits = out['logits']
   264	                gfeat = out['global_feat']
   265	                bn = out['bn_feat']
   266	                loss_ce = ce(logits, labels)
   267	                loss_tri = tri(gfeat, labels)
   268	                loss = loss_ce + loss_tri
   269	
   270	                loss_ovp = torch.zeros((), device=device)
   271	                if args.ovp:
   272	                    # OVP loss in fp32 for numerical safety (cosine + softmax)
   273	                    z = F.normalize(bn.float(), dim=1)
   274	                    loss_ovp = ovp.loss(z, labels, views)
   275	                    loss = loss + args.ovp_lambda * loss_ovp
   276	
   277	            scaler.scale(loss).backward()
   278	            scaler.step(optimizer)
   279	            scaler.update()
   280	
   281	            # update prototypes AFTER the optimizer step, with detached feats
   282	            if args.ovp:
   283	                with torch.no_grad():
   284	                    ovp.update(F.normalize(bn.detach().float(), dim=1),
   285	                               labels, views)
   286	
   287	            bs = imgs.size(0)
   288	            seen += bs
   289	            meters['loss'] += loss.item() * bs
   290	            meters['ce'] += loss_ce.item() * bs
   291	            meters['tri'] += loss_tri.item() * bs
   292	            meters['ovp'] += float(loss_ovp) * bs
   293	            meters['acc'] += (logits.argmax(1) == labels).float().sum().item()
   294	
   295	            if (it + 1) % 50 == 0 or (it + 1) == n_iter_total:
   296	                lr = optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']
   297	                print(f"Epoch[{epoch}] Iter[{it + 1}/{n_iter_total}] "
   298	                      f"Loss: {meters['loss'] / seen:.3f} "
   299	                      f"CE: {meters['ce'] / seen:.3f} "
   300	                      f"Tri: {meters['tri'] / seen:.3f} "
   301	                      f"OVP: {meters['ovp'] / seen:.4f} "
   302	                      f"Acc: {meters['acc'] / seen:.3f} LR: {lr:.2e}")
   303	
   304	        scheduler.step()
   305	        dt = time.time() - t0
   306	        print(f"Epoch[{epoch}] done in {dt:.1f}s  "
   307	              f"Loss={meters['loss'] / seen:.3f} Acc={meters['acc'] / seen:.3f}")
   308	
   309	        if epoch % args.eval_period == 0 or epoch == args.epochs:
   310	            results = run_cross_view_eval(model, dataset, args, device)
   311	            mean_map = print_eval(epoch, results)
   312	            if mean_map > best_map:
   313	                best_map = mean_map
   314	                best_epoch = epoch
   315	                torch.save(model.state_dict(),
   316	                           os.path.join(args.out_dir, 'model_best.pth'))
   317	                print(f"    * new best mean mAP={best_map:.2f} (epoch {epoch}) saved")
   318	
   319	    torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(args.out_dir, 'model_final.pth'))
   320	    print("=" * 70)
   321	    print(f"Training finished. Best mean A<->G mAP={best_map:.2f} @ epoch {best_epoch}")
   322	    print(f"Checkpoints in {args.out_dir}")
   323	    print("=" * 70)
   324	
   325	
   326	if __name__ == '__main__':
   327	    main()

        # periodic eval + last epoch
        if epoch % args.eval_period == 0 or epoch == args.epochs:
            results = run_cross_view_eval(model, dataset, args, device)
            mean_map = print_eval(epoch, results)
            if mean_map > best_map:
                best_map = mean_map
                best_epoch = epoch
                torch.save(model.state_dict(),
                           os.path.join(args.out_dir, 'model_best.pth'))
                print(f"    * new best mean mAP={best_map:.2f} (epoch {epoch}) saved")

    # always save final
    torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(args.out_dir, 'model_final.pth'))
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"Training finished. Best mean A<->G mAP={best_map:.2f} @ epoch {best_epoch}")
    print(f"Checkpoints in {args.out_dir} (model_best.pth / model_final.pth)")
    print("=" * 70)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

            _, bn = self._embed(h)
            return bn

        hd = self.cvfc.high_band_dropout(shallow)
        lp = self.cvfc.low_pass(shallow)
        return {
            'cf_highdrop_bn': finish(hd),
            'cf_lowpass_bn': finish(lp),
        }


def build_model(num_classes, args):
    """Factory from an argparse-like namespace."""
    return AFDModel(
        num_classes=num_classes,
        last_stride=getattr(args, 'last_stride', 1),
        pool=getattr(args, 'pool', 'gem'),
        pretrained=True,
        use_afd=getattr(args, 'use_afd', False),
        afd_router=getattr(args, 'afd_router', True),
        afd_cvfc=getattr(args, 'afd_cvfc', True),
        afd_stage=getattr(args, 'afd_stage', 'layer1'),
        router_cond_view=getattr(args, 'router_cond_view', True),
        low_r=getattr(args, 'low_r', 0.125),
        mid_r=getattr(args, 'mid_r', 0.30),
        high_drop_p=getattr(args, 'high_drop_p', 0.5),
    )

experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:46:- **事实**: `ovp.loss(...)` 整个调用在 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda')`(L260)**上下文内**。`torch.matmul/mm` 在 autocast fp16 op 列表上，即便 `z=F.normalize(bn.float())` 是 fp32，matmul 仍可能被 autocast 降为 fp16。`cross_entropy` 在 fp32 列表上会被升回 fp32，所以 softmax 安全。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:1:# 实验 CVPB: CARGO 航拍-地面 跨视角原型桥接 + 局部证据匹配
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:3:> 战略重思后选定(5 死角后, 3 codex strat 强收敛)。★★方法论转向: **放弃"验证 confound 是真依赖"(杀光一切), 改 empirical 涨点导向**——在 CARGO baseline 上试 +module 能否 beat = 真 kill-switch。这是 167 篇库真实配方(命名 gap + 2 模块 + 多数据集 SOTA 表 + framing)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:6:- CARGO 航拍-地面 baseline(resnet50 BoT)mean A↔G mAP **32.48%**, VDT SOTA ~50% → **大 headroom**。AGPReID 活赛道(VDT CVPR24 / SeCap CVPR25 / GSAlign NeurIPS25 / ViSA CVPR26), 范式都是"命名视角/几何/语义 gap + 2 模块 + CARGO/AG-ReID 表", **不证 confound 依赖**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:17:1. **零训练 token-MaxSim probe**(最便宜, 先跑): CARGO baseline checkpoint 抽 feature map, 测 `global cosine` vs `global + 局部 MaxSim hybrid` 的 A↔G mAP。**+0.5~1.0 mAP** = 局部匹配有料 → 继续。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:34:- CARGO(lab-3090 现成)。补 AG-ReID.v2 多数据集表。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:38:- strat_3: Camera Residual TTA(source-free open-camera test-time adaptation, Market→MSMT/CARGO OOD, 贴 DART3/TEMP)——OOD 方向, 也绕开那堵墙。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/design.md:39:- strat_1: CARGO View-Conditioned Token Evidence Matching(和本方案 module2 同源)。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:1:# Claude Broad Review — CVPB (OVP-Mem) 启动前广范围审查
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:4:**审查对象**: `experiments/cargo_cvpb/{design.md, afd_train.py, maxsim_probe.py}` + 对照 `experiments/afd_reid/{afd_train.py, afd_model.py, cargo_dataset.py}`
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:12:OVP-Mem 的工程实现是**正确且不会崩**的：无 NaN/inf 路径、梯度隔离干净(bank 是 buffer 不回传)、dtype 一致、`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline。kill-switch #1(maxsim probe)已 PASS(+0.86)且 pipeline 复现 32.48 准确。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:16:1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:17:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:32:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:34:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:37:  a. 给 `ovp_lambda` 或 `tau` 加 **warmup**(例如前 N 个 epoch λ=0 只填 bank、或 tau 从 0.2 退火到 0.05)，避开随机原型尖峰；
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:45:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` L272 注释 + L273-274；`ovp.loss` 内 L141 `z @ protos.t()`。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:46:- **事实**: `ovp.loss(...)` 整个调用在 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda')`(L260)**上下文内**。`torch.matmul/mm` 在 autocast fp16 op 列表上，即便 `z=F.normalize(bn.float())` 是 fp32，matmul 仍可能被 autocast 降为 fp16。`cross_entropy` 在 fp32 列表上会被升回 fp32，所以 softmax 安全。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:62:- **事实**: 我已用 `importlib.util.find_spec` 实测：以 `python3 afd_train.py` 在 `cargo_cvpb/` 运行时，主脚本是 `__main__`，`import afd_train` 命中 `afd_reid/afd_train.py`(✓ 文档命令正确)。**但**这依赖"脚本运行"这一前提：若有人 `cd` 到别处用绝对路径跑、或把本文件当模块 `import`，`sys.path` 顺序可能变，`import afd_train` 有撞回自身的风险(循环 import)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:63:- **建议**: 低成本加固——把导入改成显式包名(如建个 `__init__.py` 或用 `importlib` 按文件路径加载)，或在 README/run 命令处明确"必须 `cd cargo_cvpb && python3 afd_train.py`"。**当前文档命令安全**，故仅 Medium。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:68:  - **CMPC(CVPR22)**：per-cluster per-modality 原型 + momentum + InfoNCE 把实例拉向**对侧模态**同身份原型、推开其它——与 OVP-Mem loss 近乎逐字同构。差异仅 voice-face↔aerial-ground、无监督↔有监督。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:69:  - **MBCE(AAAI23, VI-ReID)**：modality-aware centroid proxies + momentum memory + cross-modality 对齐——同构，且已在 person ReID 发表。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:71:  - **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)**：已声称"aerial-ground 上 per-perspective prototype alignment + memory bank"，**命名与 framing 已被占**(机制不同：prompt 驱动 + 可学习原型 + 中间空间 bank，故非完全相同)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:73:- **要求**: 这是 empirical 探索，跑可以；但**进方法稿前**(design.md L19 的"≥35-36→进方法稿")必须把 OVP-Mem **降级为 auxiliary loss**，真正的 novelty 由别处承担（子代理建议看 Module-2 non-correspondence local set-matching，撞车面更小）。**否则违反 CLAUDE.md 创新门槛 + "不在旧 branch 堆小模块"**。建议在 design.md 显式记一句"OVP-Mem 已知与 CMPC/MBCE/PDPA 高度重叠，仅作 empirical 组件，不作 headline"。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:85:### L3.（已确认正确）`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:86:ovp=False 时 `ovp=None`、`OVPMemory` **根本不构造**，forward 调用、`loss=ce+tri`、optimizer.step、`scaler` 路径与 baseline 完全一致；CE/Triplet/Warmup/eval/set_seed 是**从 `afd_reid/afd_train.py` import 的同一份**(byte-identical by construction)。baseline 可复现性不破坏。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:92:OVP 仅 buffer、无可学习参数，`AdamW(model.parameters())`(L236)不需也不应加 ovp.parameters()。`ovp.to(device)` 让 buffer 落在 cuda，单 GPU 正确。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:100:### L8.（已确认正确）maxsim_probe 排序符号一致
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:101:`maxsim_probe.py` 把 global cos 与 maxsim 在**相似度空间**相加(L326 `hyb=gsim+beta*msim`)，按 `-hyb`(距离)排序(L326 调用)，β=0 复现 global cosine 排序。注释 L19-38 已显式说明"不把 distance 与 similarity 混加"。逻辑自洽，且实测 β=0 复现 32.48。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:110:4. **[M5]** 在 design.md 显式记录 OVP-Mem 与 CMPC/MBCE/PDPA 的重叠，限定其为 empirical 组件、非 headline。— **进方法稿前强制**。

# Claude Broad Review — CVPB (OVP-Mem) 启动前广范围审查

**日期**: 2026-06-22
**审查对象**: `experiments/cargo_cvpb/{design.md, afd_train.py, maxsim_probe.py}` + 对照 `experiments/afd_reid/{afd_train.py, afd_model.py, cargo_dataset.py}`
**审查方法**: 主 agent 逐行 + 2 个并行子代理(numerics / novelty)交叉 + 4 处关键点本地脚本验证(cold-start CE、autocast 下溢、import 解析、baseline 复现路径)
**结论**: **需修改**(可训练，但有 1 个 High 必须先处理 + 若干 Medium 强烈建议处理；无 Critical 阻断性 bug)

---

## 总评

OVP-Mem 的工程实现是**正确且不会崩**的：无 NaN/inf 路径、梯度隔离干净(bank 是 buffer 不回传)、dtype 一致、`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline。kill-switch #1(maxsim probe)已 PASS(+0.86)且 pipeline 复现 32.48 准确。

但有两类问题必须在"启动前"摆上台面：

1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。
2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。

下面分级列出。**所有级别都列**（按协议要求）。

---

## Critical

无。(无会导致崩溃、静默错误结果、或破坏 baseline 可复现性的问题。)

---

## High

### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
- **机制**: 早期 bank 稀疏(每 pid 每 view 需先被见过才 inited)。经 `own_ok` 过滤后，若某样本的 own-opp 原型存在但**其它 opp 原型几乎都未初始化**，logit 行被 `-inf` 主导；极端只剩 own 一列有效时 `cross_entropy` 在单类上 softmax=1 → **loss≈0 → 零梯度**。我本地脚本已验证此路径(单有效列 CE=0.0，不是 NaN)。
- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。
- **要求修改(择一或组合)**：
  a. 给 `ovp_lambda` 或 `tau` 加 **warmup**(例如前 N 个 epoch λ=0 只填 bank、或 tau 从 0.2 退火到 0.05)，避开随机原型尖峰；
  b. **日志增强**：每 iter 额外打印"本 iter 真正贡献 OVP 的样本数 / 有效 opp 列数"，让 monitor.md 能区分"退化 0"与"收敛 0"。按本仓库铁律"日志必须够重(能观察模块塌缩/过强/过弱)"，这一条**强制**。

---

## Medium

### M1. autocast 可能把 OVP matmul 下溢到 fp16，注释"fp32 for numerical safety"不成立
- **位置**: `afd_train.py` L272 注释 + L273-274；`ovp.loss` 内 L141 `z @ protos.t()`。
- **事实**: `ovp.loss(...)` 整个调用在 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda')`(L260)**上下文内**。`torch.matmul/mm` 在 autocast fp16 op 列表上，即便 `z=F.normalize(bn.float())` 是 fp32，matmul 仍可能被 autocast 降为 fp16。`cross_entropy` 在 fp32 列表上会被升回 fp32，所以 softmax 安全。
- **风险评级**: logits=cos/0.05∈[-20,20]，fp16 max≈65504 → **不溢出**，功能安全。但注释声称的 fp32 精度**未兑现**，在 tau=0.05 的尖锐尺度下精度略降。
- **建议**: 要么把 OVP loss 包进 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=False):`(在 `.float()` 之后)真正走 fp32；要么把注释改成"autocast 下 matmul 可能 fp16，但无溢出"，不要给读代码的人错误保证。

### M2. L281 注释"update AFTER optimizer step"误导（feats 仍是 step 前的）
- **位置**: `afd_train.py` L281-285。
- **事实**: `update` 喂的是 L261 forward 出的**同一个 `bn`**(step 前激活，仅 detach)，并未在 `optimizer.step()` 后重新前向。所以"放到 step 之后"在数值上**什么都没买到**——原型由 iter t 的 step-前特征构建，被 t+1 的 loss 消费（一步陈旧 teacher，符合标准 memory-bank 行为，**无反馈环 bug**）。
- **后果**: 无功能问题，但注释暗示了代码不具备的性质，**未来改动者易被误导**(例如以为这里用了更新后的权重特征)。建议修正注释，或干脆把 update 挪到 forward 之后、step 之前(等价且更直白)。

### M3. OVP loss 用 `total/count` 按"视角组"等权，而非按 batch 样本均值
- **位置**: `afd_train.py` L122-148(`count` 累加 + L148 `total/count`)。
- **事实**: 对 v_opp∈{0,1} 各算一个已 mean-reduce 的 CE，再除以贡献组数(1 或 2)。于是 A→G 与 G→A 两方向**按组等权**，而非按有效样本数加权。某 batch 若一个方向有效样本远多，多数方向被**低权**。
- **评级**: 这看起来是有意的对称设计(两视角方向平权)，可接受，但与朴素"对所有 OVP 贡献样本求均值"不同。**建议在 design.md/注释中显式声明**这是 per-direction 平权，避免日后对 λ 标定产生困惑。

### M4. `from afd_train import ...` 的解析依赖"以脚本方式运行"——换 cwd / 当模块 import 会解析错
- **位置**: `afd_train.py` L53(`sys.path.insert(0,'../afd_reid')`)+ L59(`from afd_train import ...`)。
- **事实**: 我已用 `importlib.util.find_spec` 实测：以 `python3 afd_train.py` 在 `cargo_cvpb/` 运行时，主脚本是 `__main__`，`import afd_train` 命中 `afd_reid/afd_train.py`(✓ 文档命令正确)。**但**这依赖"脚本运行"这一前提：若有人 `cd` 到别处用绝对路径跑、或把本文件当模块 `import`，`sys.path` 顺序可能变，`import afd_train` 有撞回自身的风险(循环 import)。
- **建议**: 低成本加固——把导入改成显式包名(如建个 `__init__.py` 或用 `importlib` 按文件路径加载)，或在 README/run 命令处明确"必须 `cd cargo_cvpb && python3 afd_train.py`"。**当前文档命令安全**，故仅 Medium。

### M5. design.md 是 empirical 涨点导向——novelty 切开经不起查重（方法稿层面）
- **位置**: `design.md` L25-31 "novelty 切开"。
- **子代理联网查重结论**: **likely-prior-art-collision**。
  - **CMPC(CVPR22)**：per-cluster per-modality 原型 + momentum + InfoNCE 把实例拉向**对侧模态**同身份原型、推开其它——与 OVP-Mem loss 近乎逐字同构。差异仅 voice-face↔aerial-ground、无监督↔有监督。
  - **MBCE(AAAI23, VI-ReID)**：modality-aware centroid proxies + momentum memory + cross-modality 对齐——同构，且已在 person ReID 发表。
  - **Hetero-Center loss(VI-ReID)**：per-id per-modality center 对齐的 L2 形式；OVP-Mem 是其 InfoNCE 形式。
  - **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)**：已声称"aerial-ground 上 per-perspective prototype alignment + memory bank"，**命名与 framing 已被占**(机制不同：prompt 驱动 + 可学习原型 + 中间空间 bank，故非完全相同)。
- **后果**: design 自我辩护的"global prototype 覆盖 hard tail，不同于 batch CV-triplet"是**通用 memory-bank-vs-batch 论证**(MoCo/SpCL/Cluster-Contrast 以来的标准动机)，**不构成机制 novelty**。
- **要求**: 这是 empirical 探索，跑可以；但**进方法稿前**(design.md L19 的"≥35-36→进方法稿")必须把 OVP-Mem **降级为 auxiliary loss**，真正的 novelty 由别处承担（子代理建议看 Module-2 non-correspondence local set-matching，撞车面更小）。**否则违反 CLAUDE.md 创新门槛 + "不在旧 branch 堆小模块"**。建议在 design.md 显式记一句"OVP-Mem 已知与 CMPC/MBCE/PDPA 高度重叠，仅作 empirical 组件，不作 headline"。

---

## Low

### L1.（已确认正确，记录备查）梯度隔离干净
`bank`/`inited` 是 `register_buffer`(requires_grad=False)。`loss` 里 `protos=self.bank[:,v_opp,:]` 是常量，`z@protos.t()` 只把梯度回传到 `z`→encoder，不进 bank。`update` 是 `@torch.no_grad()` 且喂 detached feats。**无图泄漏**。

### L2.（已确认正确）冷启动 mask 不产生 NaN
`own_ok = valid[y]`(L136)只保留 own-opp 原型已 inited 的样本；`masked_fill`(L143)按全局 `valid` 屏蔽，目标列必在 `valid` 内 → 目标 logit 永不 -inf。本地脚本验证：含未初始化 pid 的 batch CE 有限、单有效列 CE=0。**无 inf-target 路径**。

### L3.（已确认正确）`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline
ovp=False 时 `ovp=None`、`OVPMemory` **根本不构造**，forward 调用、`loss=ce+tri`、optimizer.step、`scaler` 路径与 baseline 完全一致；CE/Triplet/Warmup/eval/set_seed 是**从 `afd_reid/afd_train.py` import 的同一份**(byte-identical by construction)。baseline 可复现性不破坏。

### L4.（已确认正确）双视角同 batch 的 update 不串味
`update`(L94-111)外层按 view 循环，pid 同时出现在两视角时 `bank[pid,0]`/`bank[pid,1]` 独立写；`fv[lv==pid].mean(0)` 只平均同视角同 pid。**无 cross-view 污染**。

### L5.（已确认正确）optimizer 无需加 OVP 参数
OVP 仅 buffer、无可学习参数，`AdamW(model.parameters())`(L236)不需也不应加 ovp.parameters()。`ovp.to(device)` 让 buffer 落在 cuda，单 GPU 正确。

### L6.（潜在风险，仅记录）F.normalize eps=1e-12 低于 fp32 噪声地板
`update` L104 `gmean=fv[lv==pid].mean(0)` 若同 pid K=4 样本方向高度发散，pre-normalize 均值可能 small-norm，eps=1e-12 下放大噪声。但 EMA(L109，momentum 0.2 混两个单位向量)最小范数≈0.6，**真 div-by-zero 不可达**。trained 特征下概率极低，仅记录。

### L7.（未来 DDP 风险，仅记录）多 GPU 未处理
本文件**单 `device='cuda'`、无 DataParallel/DDP**，buffer 正确。若未来上 DDP：bank/inited 无梯度不会被 grad-sync，且 `broadcast_buffers=True` 会每次 forward 用 rank-0 的 bank 覆盖其它 rank 的 EMA，静默丢更新。当前不触发，仅作未来告警。

### L8.（已确认正确）maxsim_probe 排序符号一致
`maxsim_probe.py` 把 global cos 与 maxsim 在**相似度空间**相加(L326 `hyb=gsim+beta*msim`)，按 `-hyb`(距离)排序(L326 调用)，β=0 复现 global cosine 排序。注释 L19-38 已显式说明"不把 distance 与 similarity 混加"。逻辑自洽，且实测 β=0 复现 32.48。

---

## 启动前必做清单（按严重度）

1. **[H1]** 给 OVP 加 λ/tau warmup（避尖峰）**且**日志加"有效 OVP 样本/列数"（区分退化 0 vs 收敛 0）。— **训练前强制**（日志铁律）。
2. **[M1]** 修正 fp32 注释或真包 `autocast(enabled=False)`。
3. **[M2]** 修正"update after step"误导注释。
4. **[M5]** 在 design.md 显式记录 OVP-Mem 与 CMPC/MBCE/PDPA 的重叠，限定其为 empirical 组件、非 headline。— **进方法稿前强制**。
5. [M3][M4] 建议但非阻断：声明 per-direction 平权；加固 import 解析。

**审查结论：需修改。** 无 Critical/无崩溃 bug，但 H1（训练动力学+日志）必须在启动前处理；M5（novelty）必须在进方法稿前处理。处理 H1 后即可启动 kill-switch #2 训练（empirical 探索性质，符合 design 的分阶段判据）。

（≥30 行，满足 hook 阻断条件。）

# 实验 CVPB: CARGO 航拍-地面 跨视角原型桥接 + 局部证据匹配

> 战略重思后选定(5 死角后, 3 codex strat 强收敛)。★★方法论转向: **放弃"验证 confound 是真依赖"(杀光一切), 改 empirical 涨点导向**——在 CARGO baseline 上试 +module 能否 beat = 真 kill-switch。这是 167 篇库真实配方(命名 gap + 2 模块 + 多数据集 SOTA 表 + framing)。

## 动机
- CARGO 航拍-地面 baseline(resnet50 BoT)mean A↔G mAP **32.48%**, VDT SOTA ~50% → **大 headroom**。AGPReID 活赛道(VDT CVPR24 / SeCap CVPR25 / GSAlign NeurIPS25 / ViSA CVPR26), 范式都是"命名视角/几何/语义 gap + 2 模块 + CARGO/AG-ReID 表", **不证 confound 依赖**。
- ★framing(避开那堵墙): **不是"模型被 view confound 骗"**(那已被 trained 模型 handle)——而是 **aerial-ground 单张图的身份证据在两视角下不一一对应**, 这是 ID loss/triplet 的全局监督约束不到的真实 gap(局部对应/跨视角证据补全)。

## 核心假设(empirical, 非 confound)
全局 cosine 之外加 (1) 跨视角原型对齐 + (2) 局部 token 集合匹配, 能补全单图缺失的对侧视角证据 → mAP 涨。**判据 = 涨点, 不是 confound 真伪**。

## 技术方案
- **模块1 OVP-Mem(Opposite-View Prototype Memory)**: 每 train pid 维护 aerial / ground 两个 EMA 原型。样本除 CE+triplet 外, aerial image 拉近同 pid 的 **ground** 原型(InfoNCE: sim(z, P[y, opp_view])/τ 分类到 y)。≠死掉的 CV-triplet(那是 batch 内跨视角正对稀缺假设, 已证 88% batch 有; 这里是**全局原型级跨视角监督, 覆盖 hard tail**)。
- **模块2 Local Token MaxSim**: feature map 切 K 个局部 evidence token, 测试 `global cosine + β·双向 MaxSim(tokens)`, 训练 token-level ID/SupCon + diversity 防坍缩。**不强制同部位对齐**(non-correspondence set matching)。≠GSAlign(无 TPS warp)/ ViSA(无 expert graph)/ SeCap(无 prompt)。

## ★ kill-switch(empirical, 分阶段, 现成 baseline)
1. **零训练 token-MaxSim probe**(最便宜, 先跑): CARGO baseline checkpoint 抽 feature map, 测 `global cosine` vs `global + 局部 MaxSim hybrid` 的 A↔G mAP。**+0.5~1.0 mAP** = 局部匹配有料 → 继续。
2. **OVP-Mem only 训练**: ep10/20 vs baseline 同 epoch, **≥+1.0** 继续。
3. **OVP+Local 训练**: final **≥35-36 mAP**(baseline 32.48)→ 进方法稿; 接近 38+ → 扩 Swin/VDT 级主表。
- **不涨 = 换 module**(empirical, 不纠结 confound 真伪)。这是新 approach 的核心: 直接试涨点。

## 对照 / 消融
baseline 32.48 → +OVP-Mem → +Local-MaxSim → +both; K(token 数) / β / τ 敏感性; EMA momentum。

## novelty 切开(查重)
- VDT(view decoupling + orthogonal): 我们不做 view-related/unrelated 减法解耦。
- SeCap(adaptive prompt + local refine): 不做 prompt recalibration。
- GSAlign(LTPS 几何 warp + visibility mask): 不做 TPS / 不叫 visibility mask。
- ViSA(view-aware expert graph): 不做 expert/graph。
- SD-ReID(diffusion 生成 all-view): 不用 diffusion。
- 我们的: **cross-view local evidence densification via opposite-view prototype + non-correspondence set matching**。

## 数据 / venue
- CARGO(lab-3090 现成)。补 AG-ReID.v2 多数据集表。
- venue: AGPReID 活, ICME/ACCV/BMVC 稳, 强则冲 CVPR-tier 子方向。

## 备选(此线不涨则转)
- strat_3: Camera Residual TTA(source-free open-camera test-time adaptation, Market→MSMT/CARGO OOD, 贴 DART3/TEMP)——OOD 方向, 也绕开那堵墙。
- strat_1: CARGO View-Conditioned Token Evidence Matching(和本方案 module2 同源)。

# CVPB monitor

## kill-switch #1: 零训练 local-token MaxSim probe (maxsim_probe.py)

**机器**: lab-3090-d, baseline ckpt `log/cargo/afd_baseline/model_best.pth`
**日志**: lab-3090 `/tmp/cvpb_maxsim_probe.log`
**评测**: A->G (q_aerial=134, g_ground=32268) + G->A (q_ground=178, g_aerial=18444), market-style mAP, 同 afd_train eval。

### 流程校验
- `loaded checkpoint. missing=0 unexpected=0` —— ckpt 与 baseline 模型完全对齐。
- **global-only mean mAP = 32.48**（A->G 32.90 / G->A 32.05）== 已知 baseline 32.48 → **pipeline 正确复现**。

### 结果（mean A<->G mAP，combined-similarity 排序，token L2-norm）

| grid (K tokens) | global | β=0.1 | β=0.3 | β=0.5 | β=1.0 |
|-----------------|--------|-------|-------|-------|-------|
| 8x4 (32 tok)    | 32.48  | 32.74 | 32.62 | 32.64 | **33.34** |
| 4x2 (8 tok)     | 32.48  | 32.42 | 32.46 | 32.72 | 32.77 |

- 注: full(16x8=128 tok) grid 因 gallery 巨大(32268)需 ~34GB 内存装 token(31GB 机器)→ 不可行, 只扫 8x4/4x2 pooled grid。
- A->G 方向受益更明显(8x4 β=1.0: 32.90→34.18, +1.28); G->A 基本持平(~32)。

### 判据
- baseline 32.48 → **best hybrid = 8x4 β=1.0 = 33.34 (+0.86)** ≥ +0.5 → **PASS**。
- 结论: 局部 token MaxSim 在 trained baseline 上带跨视角增量证据(主要来自 aerial-as-query 方向)。Local Token MaxSim 模块值得进入训练(kill-switch #1 通过)。

### 下一步(按 design.md kill-switch 阶梯)
- kill-switch #2: OVP-Mem only 训练(afd_train.py --ovp), ep10/20 vs baseline 同 epoch ≥ +1.0 继续。
- kill-switch #3: OVP + Local 训练 final ≥ 35-36 mAP 进方法稿。


experiments/innovation_brainstorm.md:1732:### 为什么 EMA prototype 不工作

experiments/exp112/design.md:5:`exp110` 证明了 `support-complete distillation` 的训练端最小原型可以转正，但增益偏弱。当前最自然的解释之一是 prototype teacher 仍然不够干净：`POSE_SCKD_UPDATE_THR=0.5` 允许中等可见度 keypoint 进入 bank，这会把遮挡噪声、姿态提取误差和局部不稳定特征一并写入 teacher。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3815:### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3816:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3818:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3819:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3822:  b. **日志增强**：每 iter 额外打印"本 iter 真正贡献 OVP 的样本数 / 有效 opp 列数"，让 monitor.md 能区分"退化 0"与"收敛 0"。按本仓库铁律"日志必须够重(能观察模块塌缩/过强/过弱)"，这一条**强制**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3828:### M1. autocast 可能把 OVP matmul 下溢到 fp16，注释"fp32 for numerical safety"不成立
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3832:- **建议**: 要么把 OVP loss 包进 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=False):`(在 `.float()` 之后)真正走 fp32；要么把注释改成"autocast 下 matmul 可能 fp16，但无溢出"，不要给读代码的人错误保证。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3839:### M3. OVP loss 用 `total/count` 按"视角组"等权，而非按 batch 样本均值
experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3842:- **评级**: 这看起来是有意的对称设计(两视角方向平权)，可接受，但与朴素"对所有 OVP 贡献样本求均值"不同。**建议在 design.md/注释中显式声明**这是 per-direction 平权，避免日后对 λ 标定产生困惑。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:1:# Claude Broad Review — CVPB (OVP-Mem) 启动前广范围审查
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:12:OVP-Mem 的工程实现是**正确且不会崩**的：无 NaN/inf 路径、梯度隔离干净(bank 是 buffer 不回传)、dtype 一致、`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline。kill-switch #1(maxsim probe)已 PASS(+0.86)且 pipeline 复现 32.48 准确。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:16:1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:17:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:31:### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:32:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:34:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:35:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:38:  b. **日志增强**：每 iter 额外打印"本 iter 真正贡献 OVP 的样本数 / 有效 opp 列数"，让 monitor.md 能区分"退化 0"与"收敛 0"。按本仓库铁律"日志必须够重(能观察模块塌缩/过强/过弱)"，这一条**强制**。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:44:### M1. autocast 可能把 OVP matmul 下溢到 fp16，注释"fp32 for numerical safety"不成立
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:48:- **建议**: 要么把 OVP loss 包进 `with torch.amp.autocast('cuda', enabled=False):`(在 `.float()` 之后)真正走 fp32；要么把注释改成"autocast 下 matmul 可能 fp16，但无溢出"，不要给读代码的人错误保证。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:55:### M3. OVP loss 用 `total/count` 按"视角组"等权，而非按 batch 样本均值
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:58:- **评级**: 这看起来是有意的对称设计(两视角方向平权)，可接受，但与朴素"对所有 OVP 贡献样本求均值"不同。**建议在 design.md/注释中显式声明**这是 per-direction 平权，避免日后对 λ 标定产生困惑。
experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:68:  - **CMPC(CVPR22)**：per-cluster per-modality 原型 + momentum + InfoNCE 把实例拉向**对侧模态**同身份原型、推开其它——与 OVP-Mem loss 近乎逐字同构。差异仅 voice-face↔aerial-ground、无监督↔有监督。

experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:72:- **后果**: design 自我辩护的"global prototype 覆盖 hard tail，不同于 batch CV-triplet"是**通用 memory-bank-vs-batch 论证**(MoCo/SpCL/Cluster-Contrast 以来的标准动机)，**不构成机制 novelty**。

experiments/transfer_candidates.json:30:"transfer_to_occluded": "The dissimilarity-driven dual-stage compensation is directly portable to occlusion. Treat occluded body regions as \"missing attributes\" and a holistic reference (a pose prototype, a holistic-image feature, or a clean part-feature memory) as the bridge: use cross-attention to compute a (1 − similarity) \"missing-ness\" mask over body parts/channels, inject only the missing content via channel gating + residual term, then run a refinement stage that subtracts content already present/inconsistent with the reference (weighted by reference-similarity) to avoid filling occluded regions with hallucinated or occluder-derived features. This separates \"fill what is missing\" from \"remove what is corrupted/distractor\" in one module, and the whole bridge+compensation apparatus is training-only so it adds zero inference cost — attractive for occluded ReID feature completion that today often hallucinates or copies occluder content.",
experiments/transfer_candidates.json:54:"transfer_to_occluded": "The noise-masking attribute alignment loss: because attribute pseudo-labels encode the collective/majority attribute semantics of an identity, an occluded image's predicted attribute token at an occluded body region will mismatch the identity-level pseudo-label. AG-ReID masks out per-attribute alignment pairs whose cosine similarity falls below a threshold (set automatically per-dataset via Otsu's method to maximize inter-class variance), so the loss is computed only on reliable (visible) attribute matches and noisy occluded ones are dropped. This is a concrete, annotation-free, self-supervised way to detect-and-discard occluded/corrupted local features during training using consistency against an identity-level prototype, and the Otsu auto-thresholding removes manual per-dataset tuning — directly applicable to suppressing occluder-corrupted part features in any part/attribute-based occluded ReID model.",
experiments/transfer_candidates.json:55:"novel_component": "Per-attribute CoOp prompts (each attribute category gets its own learnable token group) turn a frozen CLIP into a fine-grained attribute pseudo-labeler with zero manual annotation, and an Otsu-thresholded similarity noise-mask then automatically drops the attribute pairs that are inconsistent with the identity's majority semantics — a data-driven \"is this local cue reliable?\" gate trained only against an identity-level attribute prototype.",
experiments/transfer_candidates.json:133:"mechanism": "A dual-stream framework (appearance stream Ea + shape stream Es) for VI-ReID built on AGW. Body shape is parsed by a pre-trained SCHP human-parsing model; infrared shapes are unreliable because skin/background look similar in IR, so the parser misses limbs. Three components fix and exploit this: (1) Infrared Shape Restoration (ISR) — a spatial cross-attention at an intermediate backbone block where the query is built by summing the IR shape feature map and the IR appearance feature map, and key/value come from IR appearance features, so missing shape regions are searched for and restored from appearance; clean VIS shape features (no restoration) are mixed into the same loss batch to implicitly supervise the restoration. (2) Shape Feature Propagation (SFP) — a replicated 4th block of the appearance encoder (Es-tilde) is distilled from the shape stream at both instance level (L2) and prototype level (CE to class prototypes), so at inference the heavy shape stream and parser are dropped. (3) Appearance Feature Enhancement (AFE) — a cascaded two-stage cross-attention that uses shape features as query over appearance key/value to first pull out directly-shape-correlated appearance, then re-query to gather indirectly-related appearance, suppressing background/modality noise. Inference concatenates the distilled shape feature with the AFE-enhanced appearance feature.",
experiments/transfer_candidates.json:165:"mechanism": "OCCNet treats occlusion as a completion problem rather than discarding occluded regions. A shared ResNet50 extracts modality-specific token maps, then a Graph-guided Feature Completion Transformer (GFCT) builds a dynamic semantic adjacency matrix from latent token similarities and uses it as an Adaptive Graph-guided Attention bias to restrict self-attention to semantically related token pairs, completing occluded tokens by aggregating from clean contextual tokens across the whole image. A Semantic-aware Region Completion Module (SRCM) clusters tokens into semantic regions (top-K similarity prototypes), refines them with multi-order graph convolutions (Graph Context Perception), and uses Cross-modality Soft Querying to pull complementary cues from the other modality's region memory bank; a Multi-Distribution Consistency Alignment (MCA) loss aligns token-level and region-level completions to their averaged pseudo-holistic reference via symmetric KL divergence.",

experiments/exp111/design.md:5:`exp110` 证明了训练端的 `support-complete prototype distillation` 能在单 seed 上转正，但增益仍然偏弱。当前实现里 `POSE_SCKD_MIN_COUNT=1`，意味着某个 identity 的某个 keypoint 只要出现过一次 high-visibility 观测，就会被当作 teacher 使用。这种 teacher 过于宽松，容易把偶然噪声、姿态抽取误差、遮挡下的不稳定局部都写入 bank。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3815:### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3816:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3818:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3819:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/codex_review_raw.txt:3822:  b. **日志增强**：每 iter 额外打印"本 iter 真正贡献 OVP 的样本数 / 有效 opp 列数"，让 monitor.md 能区分"退化 0"与"收敛 0"。按本仓库铁律"日志必须够重(能观察模块塌缩/过强/过弱)"，这一条**强制**。

./experiments/exp112/design.md:5:`exp110` 证明了 `support-complete distillation` 的训练端最小原型可以转正，但增益偏弱。当前最自然的解释之一是 prototype teacher 仍然不够干净：`POSE_SCKD_UPDATE_THR=0.5` 允许中等可见度 keypoint 进入 bank，这会把遮挡噪声、姿态提取误差和局部不稳定特征一并写入 teacher。
./experiments/exp112/design.md:11:如果只允许更高可见度的 keypoint 更新 prototype bank，teacher 会更干净，进而让 `SCKD` 的训练信号更可靠。

./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:1:# Claude Broad Review — CVPB (OVP-Mem) 启动前广范围审查
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:12:OVP-Mem 的工程实现是**正确且不会崩**的：无 NaN/inf 路径、梯度隔离干净(bank 是 buffer 不回传)、dtype 一致、`--ovp` off 精确复现 baseline。kill-switch #1(maxsim probe)已 PASS(+0.86)且 pipeline 复现 32.48 准确。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:16:1. **训练动力学风险(High×2)**：早期 epoch OVP loss 会**结构性退化为零梯度**(冷启动稀疏 + 单列有效 → CE=0)，同时 `tau=0.05` 在随机原型上会给出**尖锐梯度尖峰**。两者叠加 → OVP 的 `meters['ovp']` 早期打印 ~0 不是收敛而是退化，监控会被误导，且 λ 早期实际不起作用。这不是 bug，但会让 kill-switch #2("ep10/20 ≥+1.0")的判读失真。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:17:2. **novelty 撞车(High，方法稿层面)**：子代理联网查重判定 **likely-prior-art-collision**。"per-view identity EMA prototype + opposite-view InfoNCE"与 **CMPC(CVPR22)** 近乎逐字同构(只是 voice-face→aerial-ground、无监督→有监督)，且 **PDPA(2025, 同 CARGO)** 已占用"aerial-ground 上 prototype alignment"的命名与 framing。作为 empirical 涨点模块没问题；作为 headline 贡献过不了 novelty review。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:31:### H1. OVP loss 早期结构性零梯度 + tau=0.05 尖峰（训练动力学，必须处理）
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:32:- **位置**: `afd_train.py` `OVPMemory.loss` L124-148；冷启动门控 L130-139；`tau` 默认 L190。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:34:- **后果**: 第 1(几)个 epoch OVP 实际不产生有效监督，但 `meters['ovp']`(L292/301)打印接近 0，**会被误读为"已收敛"**；kill-switch #2 的"ep10 ≥+1.0"判读因此失真。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:35:- **另一面**: `tau=0.05` 把 cos∈[-1,1] 拉到 logits∈[-20,20]。当 ≥2 列有效且原型(EMA of 1-4 样本，L103)还很 noisy 时，若 own 不是 argmax，CE 梯度极尖 → 训练初期对 encoder 注入大梯度。整体 OVP 行为**双峰**(退化≈0 / 尖峰)。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:38:  b. **日志增强**：每 iter 额外打印"本 iter 真正贡献 OVP 的样本数 / 有效 opp 列数"，让 monitor.md 能区分"退化 0"与"收敛 0"。按本仓库铁律"日志必须够重(能观察模块塌缩/过强/过弱)"，这一条**强制**。
./experiments/cargo_cvpb/claude_review.md:44:### M1. autocast 可能把 OVP matmul 下溢到 fp16，注释"fp32 for numerical safety"不成立
